<p dir="auto">The <code>pytest</code> framework makes it easy to write small tests, yet
scales to support complex functional testing for applications and libraries.</p>
<p dir="auto">An example of a simple test:</p>
<div class="highlight highlight-source-python notranslate position-relative overflow-auto" dir="auto" data-snippet-clipboard-copy-content="# content of test_sample.py
def inc(x):
    return x + 1


def test_answer():
    assert inc(3) == 5"><pre><span class="pl-c"># content of test_sample.py</span>
<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">inc</span>(<span class="pl-s1">x</span>):
    <span class="pl-k">return</span> <span class="pl-s1">x</span> <span class="pl-c1">+</span> <span class="pl-c1">1</span>


<span class="pl-k">def</span> <span class="pl-en">test_answer</span>():
    <span class="pl-k">assert</span> <span class="pl-en">inc</span>(<span class="pl-c1">3</span>) <span class="pl-c1">==</span> <span class="pl-c1">5</span></pre></div>
<p dir="auto">To execute it:</p>
<pre>$ pytest
============================= test session starts =============================
collected 1 items

test_sample.py F

================================== FAILURES ===================================
_________________________________ test_answer _________________________________

    def test_answer():
&gt;       assert inc(3) == 5
E       assert 4 == 5
E        +  where 4 = inc(3)

test_sample.py:5: AssertionError
========================== 1 failed in 0.04 seconds ===========================
</pre>
<p dir="auto">Thanks to <code>pytest</code>'s detailed assertion introspection, you can simply use plain <code>assert</code> statements. See <a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/getting-started.html#our-first-test-run" rel="nofollow">getting-started</a> for more examples.</p>
<a name="user-content-features"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Features</h2><a id="user-content-features" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Features" href="#features"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<ul dir="auto">
<li>Detailed info on failing <a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/assert.html" rel="nofollow">assert statements</a> (no need to remember <code>self.assert*</code> names)</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/explanation/goodpractices.html#python-test-discovery" rel="nofollow">Auto-discovery</a>
of test modules and functions</li>
<li><a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/explanation/fixtures.html" rel="nofollow">Modular fixtures</a> for
managing small or parametrized long-lived test resources</li>
<li>Can run <a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/how-to/unittest.html" rel="nofollow">unittest</a> (or trial)
test suites out of the box</li>
<li>Python 3.10+ or PyPy3</li>
<li>Rich plugin architecture, with over 1300+ <a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/latest/reference/plugin_list.html" rel="nofollow">external plugins</a> and thriving community</li>
</ul>
<a name="user-content-documentation"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Documentation</h2><a id="user-content-documentation" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Documentation" href="#documentation"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">For full documentation, including installation, tutorials and PDF documents, please see <a href="https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/" rel="nofollow">https://docs.pytest.org/en/stable/</a>.</p>
<a name="user-content-bugs-requests"></a>
<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h2 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Bugs/Requests</h2><a id="user-content-bugsrequests" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Bugs/Requests" href="#bugsrequests"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
<p dir="auto">Please use the <a href="https://github.com/pytest-dev/pytest/issues">GitHub issue tracker</a> to submit bugs or request features.</p>

<div class="markdown-heading" dir="auto"><h3 tabindex="-1" class="heading-element" dir="auto">Security</h3><a id="user-content-security" class="anchor" aria-label="Permalink: Security" href="#security"><svg data-component="Octicon" class="octicon octicon-link" viewBox="0 0 16 16" version="1.1" width="16" height="16" aria-hidden="true"><path d="m7.775 3.275 1.25-1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1 4.95 4.95l-2.5 2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1-4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1 .018-1.042.751.751 0 0 1 1.042-.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l2.5-2.5a2.002 2.002 0 0 0-2.83-2.83l-1.25 1.25a.751.751 0 0 1-1.042-.018.751.751 0 0 1-.018-1.042Zm-4.69 9.64a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 2.83 0l1.25-1.25a.751.751 0 0 1 1.042.018.751.751 0 0 1 .018 1.042l-1.25 1.25a3.5 3.5 0 1 1-4.95-4.95l2.5-2.5a3.5 3.5 0 0 1 4.95 0 .751.751 0 0 1-.018 1.042.751.751 0 0 1-1.042.018 1.998 1.998 0 0 0-2.83 0l-2.5 2.5a1.998 1.998 0 0 0 0 2.83Z"></path></svg></a></div>
